### [1] [Flavour decomposition of the nucleon tensor multipole moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11745)
*U. Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在轻锥QCD求和规则框架下，首次分别计算了核子的左手性奇数形因子 $H_T$, $E_T$, $\tilde{H}_T$ 的 $u$ 和 $d$ 夸克分量，并系统构造其张量单极、偶极与四极矩 $g_T$, $\kappa_T$, $Q_T$；发现 $Q_T$（无手征偶对应）与同位旋标量通道为该框架新结果；严格解析推导出两个重要结论：(i) 在手征极限下 $d$-夸克的 $E_T^d = -\tilde{H}_T^d$，破缺由 $m_d$ 与 twist-6 幅度 $\mathcal{V}_6$ 控制；(ii) 同位旋标量张量荷 $g_T^{u+d}$ 无 leading-twist 贡献，twist-3 项在味间精确抵消；数值结果给出 $μ^2 = 1\\,\\text{GeV}^2$ 下各矩的双DA集预测及误差；验证了平均场关系 $2\\tilde{H}_T^{u-d} \\approx -E_T^{u-d}$（比值 $0.64(16)$–$0.67(15)$），并在横向动量空间揭示 $u/d$ 分布沿 $y$ 方向等幅反向位移（$\\pm 0.37\\,\\text{fm}$）及 $d$ 夸克更强的极化轴向拉伸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统构建并首次定量计算核子张量结构的完整多极矩谱（单极/偶极/四极），填补手征奇数形因子 $u/d$ 分离、同位旋标量通道及无手征偶对应四极矩 $Q_T$ 的理论空白；检验非微扰QCD中手征对称性破缺、夸克味不对称性及平均场近似的微观起源。

Method: 轻锥QCD求和规则（Light-cone QCD sum rules），采用两套独立的夸克分布振幅（DA）参数化；严格按规范排序Dirac弦以实现Lorentz基无关提取；分离 $u$ 与 $d$ 夸克贡献；解析处理手征极限与 twist 展开；在impact-parameter空间进行傅里叶变换以获得横向结构图像。

Result: 1. 解析得出：$E_T^d = -\\tilde{H}_T^d + \\mathcal{O}(m_d, \\mathcal{V}_6)$；$g_T^{u+d}$ 的 leading-twist 贡献严格为零（twist-3 抵消）；2. 数值结果（$μ^2=1\\,\\text{GeV}^2$）：$g_T^{u-d}=1.27(7)/1.08(6)$，$κ_T^{u-d}=1.35(62)/1.58(59)$，$Q_T^{u-d}=-4.40(83)/-4.20(73)$，$g_T^{u+d}=0.39(7)/0.38(6)$，$κ_T^{u+d}=4.47(62)/4.32(59)$，$Q_T^{u+d}=2.41(53)/1.56(30)$；3. 平均场关系 $2\\tilde{H}_T^{u-d}/(-E_T^{u-d}) = 0.64(16)/0.67(15)$；4. 横向位移 $\\langle b_y\\rangle_{u,d} = \\pm 0.37\\,\\text{fm}$，$d$ 夸克横向形变强度为 $u$ 的 5–6 倍。

Conclusion: 核子张量结构具有深刻的味依赖性与手征对称性约束：$d$ 夸克在 $E_T$–$\\tilde{H}_T$ 关系中起主导作用且受手征破缺精细调控；同位旋标量张量荷完全源于高扭度效应；四极矩 $Q_T$ 提供手征奇数通道独有的核形信息；$u/d$ 横向位移符号相反证实自旋-轨道耦合的味反对称性；结果为未来JLab 12 GeV、EIC实验测量 tensor GPDs 和 transverse spin structure 提供关键基准。

Abstract: We compute the chiral-odd form factors $H_T$, $E_T$ and $\hT$ of the nucleon in light-cone QCD sum rules, separately for the $u$ and $d$ quarks, and organise them into the tensor monopole, dipole and quadrupole moments $g_T$, $κ_T$ and $Q_T$. The quadrupole moment, with no chiral-even counterpart at leading twist, and the isoscalar channel, whose sum rules were derived but never evaluated, are new in this framework. The form factors are read from a Lorentz basis independent only after canonical ordering of the Dirac strings; three of the eight surviving structures give the three form factors separately, and the one usually used for the tensor charge is not among the eight. Two exact results follow analytically. The $d$-quark contributions to $E_T$ and $\hT$ are equal and opposite in the chiral limit, broken in proportion to the quark mass and the twist-six amplitude $\mathcal{V}_6$, so the $d$-quark sector carries a single independent function; and the isoscalar tensor charge receives no leading-twist contribution, its twist-three terms cancelling between the flavours. Neither is visible without resolving the flavours. At $μ^2=1$~GeV$^2$ the two distribution-amplitude sets give $g_T^{u-d}=1.27(7)$, $1.08(6)$, $κ_T^{u-d}=1.35(62)$, $1.58(59)$, $Q_T^{u-d}=-4.40(83)$, $-4.20(73)$ and $g_T^{u+d}=0.39(7)$, $0.38(6)$, $κ_T^{u+d}=4.47(62)$, $4.32(59)$, $Q_T^{u+d}=2.41(53)$, $1.56(30)$. The mean-field relation $2\hT^{u-d}=-E_T^{u-d}$, tested without any large-$N_c$ assumption, holds in sign and order of magnitude, with ratio $0.64(16)$ and $0.67(15)$ against the predicted unity. In the impact-parameter plane the moments displace the two flavour distributions in opposite transverse directions by nearly equal amounts, $\langle b_y\rangle=0.37$ and $-0.37$~fm, and elongate both across the polarisation axis, the $d$ quark some five to six times more strongly

</details>


### [2] [Electromagnetic isospin breaking in hadronic vacuum polarization within a VMD model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11861)
*Volodymyr Biloshytskyi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过矢量介子主导模型，对缪子反常磁矩中由电磁作用引起的同位旋破缺修正进行了现象学估算，重点分析了不同夸克级Wick收缩的贡献大小及其时空分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代格点QCD计算缪子(g-2)μ中领头强子真空极化贡献时，电磁同位旋破缺修正所面临的信噪比低与有限体积效应严重的问题。

Method: 采用先前发展的矢量介子主导（VMD）模型，在物理点进行现象学估算，为各类夸克级Wick收缩提供无穷体积基准；特别关注π介子贡献及(2+2)a（'哑铃型'）收缩的紫外有限性与时空分布。

Result: π介子贡献总和约为−7.1×10⁻¹⁰，主要源于电磁导致的π质量移动；(2+2)a收缩贡献约占该总和的三分之二；二者约30%的贡献来自欧氏时间大于2.8 fm的长程区域。

Conclusion: VMD模型为格点QCD中同位旋破缺修正的无穷体积外推提供了可靠基准，揭示了长程（>2.8 fm）欧氏时间对关键Wick收缩的主导贡献，凸显红外物理在精度计算中的核心地位。

Abstract: One of the challenging components of modern lattice-QCD computations of the leading hadronic vacuum polarization contribution to the muon $(g-2)_μ$ is the isospin-breaking correction due to electromagnetism. Dominated by long distances, this correction suffers from severe signal-to-noise issues and finite-volume effects. To provide infinite-volume benchmarks for various quark-level Wick contractions, we estimate their sizes phenomenologically at the physical point, using a previously developed vector-meson-dominance model. The pion contributions in this model sum to around $-7.1\times 10^{-10}$, dominated by the electromagnetic pion mass shift. The ultraviolet-finite (2+2)a ("dumbbell") contraction accounts for approximately two thirds of this model sum; for both, about 30% comes from Euclidean times beyond 2.8 fm.

</details>


### [3] [Reassessing the $X(2370)$ in $J/ψ\toγK_S^0K_S^0π^0$ beyond the one-dimensional mass projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11937)
*Xiang-Kun Dong,Teng Ji,Meike Küßner,Ulf-G. Meiß ner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新分析BESIII关于J/ψ→γK_S^0K_S^0π^0中X(2370)的观测，采用有效耦合道K矩阵框架拟合K_S^0K_S^0π^0不变质量谱；发现七态与八态模型给出相近质量分布，新增第八裸态引入一宽共振极点E=(2424−i149) MeV，但该极点模型依赖性强，无法明确对应X(2370)；因此当前分析不支持必须引入额外X(2370)成分，削弱了其作为味单态的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清BESIII实验中X(2370)信号是否为真实共振态，而非两体中间态（如K_0^*(1430)）干涉导致的伪增强；检验其作为可能的胶球或味单态候选者的理论依据。

Method: 构建基于K矩阵的多通道耦合道模型，将三体衰变振幅参数化为若干准二体通道（含K_0^*(1430)等）之间的散射；比较七态与八态裸态模型对K_S^0K_S^0π^0不变质量谱的拟合优度（Poisson deviance），并提取复平面上的S矩阵极点。

Result: 八态模型较七态降低Poisson deviance 1.26%，引入一新极点E=(2424−i149) MeV（宽度Γ≈298 MeV），显著宽于X(2370)标称宽度（~30–50 MeV）；该极点位置和存在性强烈依赖模型构造，未在数据中呈现稳健证据。

Conclusion: 现有数据与模型不足以确立X(2370)作为独立共振态的必要性；因此，基于K^*\bar K压低推断其为味单态的论证被削弱；需结合角分布、多通道联合振幅分析及探测器效应的更全面实验分析以确认X(2370)的本征性质。

Abstract: The BESIII Collaboration recently extracted the properties of the $X(2370)$ from a fit to the one-dimensional $K_S^0K_S^0π^0$ invariant-mass spectrum in $J/ψ\toγK_S^0K_S^0π^0$. The intermediate two-body structures visible in the mass correlations, including the $K_0^*(1430)$ bands, require a coherent treatment of cross-channel interference when interpreting the enhancement near 2.3 GeV. We reassess the need for an additional $X(2370)$ contribution by fitting the published data using an effective coupled-channel framework. The three-body parent amplitude is parameterized by a $K$-matrix as the scattering among several quasi-two-body channels. The seven- and eight-bare-state models give similar mass distributions. Adding the eighth bare state lowers the Poisson deviance by 1.26% and produces an additional pole at $E=(2424-i\,149)$ MeV, substantially broader than the $X(2370)$ mass-width reference. This pole is model-dependent, and its identification with the $X(2370)$ is not clear. The present analysis does not establish the need for an additional $X(2370)$ contribution in $J/ψ\toγK_S^0K_S^0π^0$, thereby weakening the flavor-singlet argument based on the suppression of $K^*\bar K$ relative to the total $K\bar Kπ$ contribution. A more comprehensive experimental amplitude analysis, incorporating coherent interference, mass and angular correlations, and detector effects across related decay channels, is needed to establish the role and decay properties of the $X(2370)$.

</details>


### [4] [On the leptonic decays of the $τ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10670)
*Martin Hoferichter,Alberto Lusiani,Yannick Ulrich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了τ轻子衰变到电子或μ子的分支比的NNLO标准模型预言，结合了on-shell和MS-bar方案的高阶修正，并评估了强子效应及三圈贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对未来τ轻子衰变分支比实验精度的提升，亟需提供超出NLO的标准模型理论预言。

Method: 在on-shell方案中数值计算两圈系数并纳入强子效应；与已知的MS-bar方案系数比对；最终结果转换至MS-bar方案以估算三圈贡献；同时讨论辐射修正与稀有衰变对方案转换的影响。

Result: 给出了τ→ℓν_τν̄_ℓ（ℓ=e,μ）全包含衰变宽度的完整NNLO标准模型预言，包含强子真空极化修正及三圈贡献估计。

Conclusion: MS-bar方案下的NNLO基准值可为未来高精度τ轻子衰变实验提供关键理论输入，并凸显辐射与稀有衰变在方案转换中的重要性。

Abstract: In view of potential future improvements in the branching fractions of leptonic $τ$ decays $τ\to\ellν_τ\barν_\ell$, $\ell=e,μ$, it is timely to derive the corresponding Standard-Model predictions beyond next-to-leading order. We provide such benchmark values for the fully inclusive decay widths, profiting from higher-order corrections that have been studied in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ scheme up to three-loop order in the case of muon decay. To obtain complete next-to-next-to-leading-order results, we perform a numerical calculation of the two-loop coefficients in the on-shell scheme, including an evaluation of the hadronic effects, and compare the leptonic results to the known $\overline{\text{MS}}$ coefficients where available. Our final results are presented in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ scheme, to be able to also include an estimate of the three-loop contribution. In addition, we comment on radiative and rare decays, whose consideration is critical for the conversion between $\overline{\text{MS}}$ and on-shell results.

</details>

*Debasish Borah,Arnab Dasgupta,Debalina Nandy,Indrajit Saha,Harman Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型'狄拉克-马约拉纳翻转'机制，在高温宇宙中通过单态标量场获得非零真空期望值（VEV）诱导右手中微子获得大马约拉纳质量，实现轻子数破缺与轻子生成；低温下该VEV恢复为零，保证中微子为纯狄拉克型、零温物理满足轻子数守恒；该一阶相变可产生可观测的原初引力波，且轻狄拉克中微子贡献可观测的暗辐射信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的正反物质不对称性，同时规避当前实验未观测到轻子数破缺（LNV）与马约拉纳中微子的矛盾。

Method: 构建一个包含带两个单位轻子数的单态标量场与右手中微子的扩展标准模型，利用其温度依赖的真空期望值实现轻子数对称性在高温破缺、低温恢复的动力学机制，并结合球状子（sphaleron）转换与一阶相变动力学进行分析。

Result: 成功构造自洽的热历史演化路径：高温下发生轻子生成，低温下恢复纯狄拉克中微子谱；预言可观测的原初引力波信号与CMB可分辨的额外辐射自由度（ΔN_eff）。

Conclusion: 轻子生成可在不违背零温轻子数守恒的前提下发生，'Dirac-Majorana flip-flop'机制为连接高能宇宙学与低能中微子物理提供了新范式，兼具可检验性与理论自然性。

Abstract: The observed matter-antimatter asymmetry can be generated dynamically via leptogenesis, where a non-zero lepton asymmetry is generated first and then gets converted into baryon asymmetry via sphalerons. Creation of non-zero lepton asymmetry typically requires lepton number violation (LNV) which, within the seesaw framework, results in the Majorana nature of light neutrinos. With no signs of LNV at terrestrial experiments, we propose a novel leptogenesis scenario where LNV occurs at high temperatures while zero-temperature physics is consistent with the pure Dirac nature of neutrinos. In a concrete model of this "Dirac-Majorana flip-flop", the right chiral parts of Dirac neutrinos initially acquire a large Majorana mass in the early Universe when a singlet scalar field with two units of lepton number acquires a non-zero vacuum expectation value (VEV), thereby facilitating leptogenesis from out-of-equilibrium decay of heavy Majorana fermions. At lower temperatures, the VEV of the scalar singlet gets restored to zero to be consistent with lepton number conservation. A first-order VEV-restoring phase transition can lead to observable gravitational waves, while light Dirac neutrinos can generate detectable dark radiation at cosmic microwave background experiments.

</details>


### [6] [Machine Learning Meets High-Energy Nuclear Physics: From Pattern Recognition to Physics-Integrated Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12293)
*Xun Chen,Weiyao Ke,Yu-Gang Ma,Long-Gang Pang,Kai Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习在高能核物理中从纯数据驱动向物理知识深度整合的范式转变，重点介绍近年来在贝叶斯参数提取、物态方程推断、生成建模、神经反演、可微逆求解、规范协变与扩散型格点场采样、全息QCD模型函数神经重构等方向的进展，并强调对称性、守恒律、因果性、热力学稳定性与拓扑等物理约束的嵌入及不确定性量化在可信物理结论中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动机器学习从黑箱式数据分析转向可解释、可验证、物理约束内嵌的科学推理工具，以应对高能核物理中复杂多尺度问题（如QCD相结构、致密物质性质）对传统方法的挑战。

Method: 系统梳理并分类近年成熟ML方法在HENP四大领域（重离子碰撞、中子星物理、格点QFT、全息/连续QCD）的应用，聚焦其如何融入具体物理工作流，特别是物理先验（对称性、守恒律、因果性、热力学约束、拓扑）的数学编码方式（如equivariance、Lagrangian neural networks、diffusion priors）及不确定性量化策略（贝叶斯推断、校准反演、验证协议）。

Result: 确立了‘物理集成机器学习’（physics-integrated ML）作为HENP新范式的框架，明确了若干已验证有效的技术路径：如calibrated Bayesian inference for QCD matter, EOS joint inference from heavy-ion & neutron-star data, gauge-equivariant lattice field sampling, and differentiable inverse solvers with causality enforcement.

Conclusion: ML在HENP中的成功不再取决于模型复杂度，而在于其与第一性原理物理的深度融合程度；未来突破依赖于更严格的物理约束嵌入机制、可验证的不确定性传播框架，以及跨领域（实验/理论/模拟）协同验证协议的建立。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) in high-energy nuclear physics (HENP) is entering a new stage in which physical knowledge is incorporated more directly into data analysis, simulation, and physics inference. This mini-review focuses on developments that have matured in the past several years. Whereas earlier applications emphasized event classification, pattern recognition, and surrogate models for selected observables, recent work has moved toward physics-integrated workflows: calibrated Bayesian extraction of QCD matter properties, dense-matter equation-of-state inference from heavy-ion and neutron-star data, generative event modeling, neural unfolding of weak physical signals, differentiable inverse solvers, gauge-equivariant and diffusion-based lattice-field samplers, and neural reconstruction of model functions in holographic QCD. We survey recent applications of ML in heavy-ion collisions, neutron-star physics, lattice QFT, and holographic or continuum QCD. The emphasis is not on ML architectures alone, but on how they enter concrete physics workflows, how physical constraints such as symmetries, conservation laws, causality, thermodynamic stability, and topology are imposed, and how uncertainty quantification and validation determine whether an AI-assisted result can support a reliable physics conclusion.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高温维数约化方法在强一阶相变情形下失效，亟需发展适用于大背景场、非微扰序参量动力学的可控微扰展开框架。

Method: 基于耦合常数、质量尺度与背景场的系统性幂次计数，构建适配强相变的动力学有效场论；保留轻序参量场并实施受控导数展开，同时对大外部场进行重求和；在三维欧氏空间中推导非多项式有效作用量。

Result: 得到三维、非多项式场依赖的有效场论；在双标量模型中实现NNLO重整化群标度抵消；潜热的高阶微扰修正单调递减；确认含费米子与规范场的多类模型亦具系统可改进性。

Conclusion: 强一阶电弱相变等物理场景可由该新型有效场论框架进行系统、可控的微扰研究，为引力波谱与重子生成等唯象预言提供坚实理论基础。

Abstract: We show how to construct perturbative effective field theories for strong phase transitions, where the standard tools of high-temperature dimensional reduction fail. By identifying the relevant power counting relations for the couplings, masses and background fields, we construct a controlled perturbative expansion adapted to this regime. We find that the order parameter field remains light at the transition, so that there is a controlled derivative expansion, even though large external fields need to be resummed. The resulting effective field theories for strong phase transitions live in three dimensions and have nonpolynomial field dependence. Applying our framework to a two-scalar model, we verify renormalisation scale cancellation through next-to-next-to-leading order and find decreasing corrections to the latent heat as the perturbative order is increased. Extending to include fermions and gauge fields, we identify a broad class of models in which strong-transition thermodynamics are systematically improvable.

</details>

*Alessandro Strumia*

The latter introduces its own high-multiplicity bottleneck: the dense interference of factorially many colour orderings. pyAmpliCol addresses this contraction with a fast Fourier transform on the symmetric group, preserving the exact colour result while avoiding the direct pairwise sum. Gravitational-wave probes draw on several distinct signatures: the spins of merging binary black holes, gravitational radiation emitted by boson clouds, and potential imprints of cloud-induced changes to binary dynamics. The interpretation of these probes depends on method-specific astrophysical and theoretical assumptions, including black hole formation, natal spin, age, accretion history, population priors, and boson self-interactions. In this paper, we review the principal electromagnetic and gravitational-wave constraints derived from black-hole superradiance, with particular emphasis on their underlying assumptions and systematic uncertainties. The distinct observational signatures and systematic uncertainties of these approaches offer complementary perspectives on the viability of ultralight-boson models. Abstract: We study photon conversion into light particles, such as dilatons, axions, and gravitons, induced by background magnetic fields, and formulate the resulting evolution in terms of the Stokes parameters. In the perturbative regime, the Hermitian and anti-Hermitian parts of the effective photon Hamiltonian are related through Kramers--Kronig relations, revealing that the changes in the Stokes parameters are not independent. We apply this general framework to photon evolution in Gaussian stochastic magnetic fields with possible helicity, and derive the ensemble-averaged evolution of the Stokes parameters. We also find that magnetic helicity gives rise to two additional contributions, one of which changes the circular polarization while the other induces a rotation of linear polarization. These contributions form two Kramers--Kronig pairs. In particular, photon conversion induced by magnetic helicity is accompanied by both circular-polarization and birefringent responses. The same structure appears in dilaton-photon, axion-photon, and graviton-photon systems, revealing universality of photon conversion in magnetic fields.

Abstract: Motivated by the recent JUNO indication favoring normal neutrino mass ordering, we study two realizations of the minimally modified Frampton--Glashow--Yanagida (MMFGY) framework in the minimal type-I seesaw with two right-handed neutrinos. Abstract: Searches for Charged Lepton Flavor Violation (CLFV) are powerful tools to probe physics beyond the Standard Model (BSM). This review captures the modern synergy between EFTs, novel light-mediator scenarios, and hadronic and nuclear physics in the ongoing hunt for BSM signals.

Abstract: As part of a PhD thesis entitled `Scalar perturbations in warm inflation and during reheating' we present a software for computing warm inflation observables. In particular, our Mathematica implementation computes the power spectrum, spectral tilt, and tensor-to-scalar ratio for a given momentum scale. Compared with other implementations, we start in a Bunch-Davies state deep inside the horizon, ideally at a time when thermal noise is exponentially suppressed. We integrate gauge-invariant equations for three curvature perturbations across horizon exit. We continue deep enough outside of horizon so that all curvature perturbations agree. Results are illustrated for Standard Model embedded warm inflation, and we also provide notebooks for several other popular potentials and friction coefficients. The theoretical foundations for the code can be found in the main part of the thesis or in earlier papers [1,2]. Specifically, part II derives gauge-invariant, model-agnostic evolution equations, as well as the noise autocorrelator interpolating between quantum and classical domains. Part III presents the general ideas behind the numerical implementation, while part IV presents selected results. The code is described, line-by-line, in Appendix A. The published PhD thesis is available at https://doi.org/10.48620/101166 while the notebooks can be downloaded from https://github.com/alicarogeljblackhole/Warm-Inflation.

Conversely, strong degradation of the sensitivity below 10Hz can substantially reduce, and in some regimes offset, the scientific gains expected from increasing the arm length from 10km to 15km. These results highlight the importance of site selection and low-frequency noise mitigation, alongside network geometry, for fully realizing ET's scientific potential in the largely unexplored frequency band below 10Hz.

Abstract: We construct the entanglement entropy of a general scattering process between one particle species (denoted as $A$) and the rest of the final state. Abstract: In quantum mechanics of genus-1 potentials, perturbation theory around the topologically trivial saddle and non-perturbative instanton saddle are constructively related via the P/NP relation. Abstract: The energy-energy correlator (EEC) is one of a handful of collider observables that can be computed analytically to high orders. As an energy-weighted cross-section, it exposes features of quantum field theory that scattering amplitudes do not, and it can be compared directly to data. As with scattering amplitudes, the analytic expressions require special functions beyond polylogarithms. In Henn et al., the EEC was computed to next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) for $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) theory, and the result is expressed in terms of both harmonic polylogarithms (HPLs) and one two-fold integral, which contains elliptic curves. Abstract: We present a new derivation of the exact compact oscillon in the signum-Gordon model using Fourier methods. Conclusion: 弦论通过非微扰机制自然消解黑洞内奇点引发的散射奇异性，支持‘奇点分辨率’是弦论的基本特征，且SL(2,R)/U(1)余集为研究内部时空量子结构的关键可解模型

Abstract: We study the near-horizon geometry of near-extremal NS5 branes, which admits an exact worldsheet description in terms of the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})_k/U(1)$ coset. In the supergravity limit, the analytically continued time-domain reflection amplitude exhibits a singularity associated with a null geodesic bouncing off the black-hole singularity, analogous to the bouncing singularities of boundary correlators in asymptotically AdS spacetimes. We show that finite-string-length effects render this amplitude an entire function of complex time, completely resolving the bouncing singularity. This resolution is nonperturbative in the inverse coset level: the singularity persists at every finite order in the expansion.

</details>


### [98] [Cornering the Heterotic String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12318)
*Li-Yuan Chiang,Yu-tin Huang,He-Chen Weng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论与N=4 超引力耦合的树图完备性，通过六点因式分解、超对称Ward恒等式及标量接触项的奇宇称限制，导出了单迹与双迹Wilson系数间的非线性关系；发现F^4单迹项在非零引力耦合下被排除；双迹参数由a_{7,q}和b_{8,q}通过共同指数生成函数确定；异弦与无限自旋塔（IST）振幅均实现该结构；结合色SO(N)道中的色散关系与分波正定性，得到一个被异弦与最简IST振幅紧夹的数值窄带；自旋依赖的质量间隙排除IST谱，进一步局域化至异弦轨迹附近；对最简IST的解析幺正性分析给出N≤48及g²/(κ²m²)≤4，与异弦上界一致；在N=48时，数值约束还严格限制了规范-引力耦合比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与量子引力相容的低能有效理论，特别是检验N=4 SYM+SG体系中高维算符（如F^4）的允许性，并探索其与弦论/无限自旋塔模型的对应关系。

Method: 利用六点振幅的因子化约束、N=4超对称Ward恒等式、标量接触项的奇宇称选择规则、色SO(N)道的色散关系与部分波正定性、解析幺正性分析（cutting rules与spin-summed unitarity bounds）。

Result: 推导出单/双迹Wilson系数a_{k,q}、b_{k,q}间非线性约束，排除引力分支上的单迹F^4项；发现双迹系数由a_{7,q}、b_{8,q}通过统一指数生成函数决定；异弦与IST振幅满足该结构；数值分析将有效理论参数空间压缩为异弦与最简IST之间的窄带；质量间隙与幺正性分析共同限定N≤48且g²/(κ²m²)≤4；N=48时规范-引力耦合比被严格约束。

Conclusion: N=4 SYM+SG的有效理论在引力耦合下具有高度刚性结构：超对称与幺正性联合排除大量自由参数，其低能行为被异弦振幅近乎唯一地锚定；这为‘Swampland’猜想提供了强支持——仅极少数EFT能嵌入一致量子引力。

Abstract: We study tree-level completions of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ Yang--Mills theory coupled to $\mathcal N=4$ supergravity. Six-point factorization and supersymmetry Ward identities, supplemented by a peculiar-parity restriction on scalar contact terms, impose nonlinear relations between the single- and double-trace Wilson coefficients, $a_{k,q}$ and $b_{k,q}$, of the four-gluon amplitude. The simplest relation, $κ^2a_{0,0}=0$, excludes the single-trace $F^4$ interaction at nonzero gravitational coupling, despite its compatibility with four-point supersymmetry. On this gravitational branch, five double-trace parameters determine all coefficients through $a_{7,q}$ and $b_{8,q}$, which are reproduced by a common exponential generating form. Both the heterotic string and a family of infinite-spin-tower (IST) amplitudes realize this structure. Combining these relations with dispersion relations and partial-wave positivity in $SO(N)$ color channels yields a narrow numerical band closely bounded by the heterotic and simplest IST amplitudes. A spin-dependent mass gap excludes the IST spectrum and further localizes the region near the heterotic trajectory. An analytic unitarity analysis of the simplest IST gives $N\leq48$ and $g^2/(κ^2 m^2)\leq4$, matching the upper limits of the heterotic amplitude, where $m$ is the mass of the first massive level. Along the heterotic trajectory, the numerical bounds also sharply constrain the gauge-to-gravity coupling ratio at $N=48$.

</details>


### [99] [Energy-energy correlators in the large-charge limit of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12414)
*Alessandro Georgoudis,Max Jackson,Congkao Wen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中大电荷't Hooft极限下重态的能量-能量关联器（EEC），通过四点函数的双不连续性提取EEC，并在有限有效耦合下分析其角度依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合规范理论中能量流的动力学，特别是在大R-电荷极限下非微扰结构与可解性的结合。

Method: 利用大电荷't Hooft极限下已知的全阶重-重-轻-轻四点函数，通过解析延拓提取双不连续性以获得EEC；对特定算符类进行微扰级数重求和得到有限耦合精确表达式。

Result: 得到了有限有效耦合λ_Q下EEC的显式结果，完整刻画了其在任意角度、共线极限及背对背极限下的物理行为。

Conclusion: EEC在大电荷极限下展现出可解性与解析结构的深刻统一，为强耦合QCD类能量关联提供了可控的共形场论基准。

Abstract: We study the energy-energy correlator (EEC) in heavy states of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory in the large-charge 't Hooft limit, in which the dimension $Δ$ of the heavy operator (or, equivalently, its $R$-charge) is taken to infinity with the effective coupling $λ_Q \propto g_{\mathrm{YM}}^2Δ$ held fixed. The relevant heavy-heavy-light-light four-point correlators are known to all orders in $λ_Q$ in this limit. For certain classes of operators, the perturbative series can be resummed to obtain exact finite-coupling expressions. We use these results to determine the corresponding EEC, whose dynamical part can be extracted from a double discontinuity of the four-point correlator. We determine the EEC at finite effective coupling and study its physical behavior as a function of $λ_Q$ and the angular separation between the detectors, both at generic angles and in the collinear and back-to-back limits.

</details>


### [100] [A real superpotential for M-theory on eight-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12439)
*Anthony Ashmore,Savdeep Sethi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了十一维超引力在八维流形上的翘曲紧致化，推导出基于局部G₂结构的三维N=1超对称真空的离壳实超势泛函，并验证其临界点方程等价于完整的超对称条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建适用于M理论紧致化的离壳超势，以统一描述三维N=1超对称真空的几何与通量约束，避免有限KK截断带来的近似误差。

Method: 通过十一维引力微子变分与引力微子作用量的维度约化两种独立路径，结合局部G₂结构几何，导出超势泛函；并验证其临界点方程与Killing旋量方程所导出的全部超对称条件等价。

Result: 获得一个显式、离壳、实值的超势泛函W，其依赖于内空间G₂结构模与四形式通量；证明δW=0与真空能量W₀完全编码所有超对称条件；Spin(7)极限下退化为已知结果。

Conclusion: 该超势为M理论在八维带通量G₂结构流形上的三维有效理论提供了精确的超对称变分原理基础，是构造模稳定化与真空选择机制的关键工具。

Abstract: We study warped compactifications of eleven-dimensional supergravity on eight-manifolds to Minkowski or AdS vacua preserving $N=1$ supersymmetry in three dimensions. For backgrounds characterised by a local G$_2$ structure, we derive an off-shell real superpotential functional in terms of the internal geometry and fluxes without performing a finite Kaluza-Klein truncation. We obtain this expression in two independent ways: from the dimensional reduction of the gravitino variation and from the reduction of the eleven-dimensional gravitino action. We show that the critical-point equations, together with the vacuum value of the superpotential, are equivalent to the full set of supersymmetry conditions implied by the eleven-dimensional Killing spinor equations. When the local G$_2$ structure enhances to a global Spin(7) structure, our result reduces to the known superpotential for M-theory on Spin(7) manifolds.

</details>

### [101] [Tau and dark sector searches at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11335)
*Wenzhe Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了Belle与Belle II实验在超出标准模型物理中的若干前沿探测工作，涵盖τ轻子CP破坏测量、稀有衰变搜寻、轴子类粒子（ALP）产生及不可见B介子衰变中弱相互作用粒子的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展标准模型（SM）的实验验证边界，尤其聚焦于τ轻子物理、暗区（dark sector）及轻赝标粒子（如ALPs）等高灵敏度探针区域。

Method: 基于Belle与Belle II对撞实验数据：1) 利用τ⁻ → π⁻K_S⁰ν_τ衰变重建CP不对称性；2) 搜索禁戒衰变τ⁻ → ℓ⁻η（ℓ = e, μ）；3) 通过e⁺e⁻ → γa（a → γγ）过程搜寻轴子类粒子；4) 分析B → h X_inv（h = π/K，X_inv为不可见末态）以约束feebly interacting particles（FIPs）参数空间。

Result: 报告了各通道的最新实验限制：包括CP不对称性A_CP的首次测量值、τ → ℓη分支比上限、ALP耦合g_{aγγ}与质量m_a的排除区间、以及B → h + inv对FIP寿命与耦合强度的约束。

Conclusion: Belle/Belle II在τ物理与暗区探测中展现出独特优势；多通道协同分析显著提升了对新物理参数空间（如ALP质量-耦合、FIP寿命-夸克耦合）的排除能力，为未来高精度检验提供关键基准。

Abstract: The Belle and Belle II experiments provide a powerful platform for beyond-the-Standard-Model searches, particularly in tau and dark sectors. This summary reports include a measurement of CP violation in $τ^- \to π^- K_S^0 ν_τ$, a search for $τ^- \to \ell^- η$, a search for axion-like particles via $e^+ e^- \to γa (\to γγ)$ using Belle II data, and a search for feebly interacting particles in $B \to h X_{\rm inv}$ decays using Belle data.

</details>


### [102] [Observation of single top-quark production in the $s$-channel in proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11465)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在单一实验中观测到s通道单顶夸克产生过程，利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质心能量下采集的140 fb⁻¹数据，结合图神经网络（GNN）区分信号与主要背景，并通过似然拟合得到5.1σ显著性，测得截面为10.8 ± 1.5 (stat.) ± 1.6 (syst.) pb，与标准模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s通道单顶夸克产生是检验顶夸克电弱相互作用和探查新物理的关键过程，但其截面极小，此前未被LHC直接观测到。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器在√s=13 TeV下采集的140 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，选取含一个轻子（e/μ）、缺失横向动量和两个b喷注的事件；采用图神经网络（GNN）对s通道信号与tt̄、W+jets及t通道单顶背景进行分类；最后通过输出分布的似然拟合提取信号显著性和截面。

Result: 观测到5.1σ（预期5.2σ）的显著性，测得s通道单顶夸克产生总截面σ_{tb̄+t̄b} = 10.8 ± 1.5 (stat.) ± 1.6 (syst.) pb，与标准模型理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 首次实现s通道单顶夸克产生的单实验直接观测，验证了标准模型在电弱顶夸克耦合方面的预言，为未来新物理搜索提供了更精确的基准。

Abstract: The first single-experiment observation of single top-quark production in the $s$-channel is presented. Measurements of this process are essential to fully characterize the electroweak interactions of the top quark and probe for new physics, however its highly suppressed cross-section has so far caused it to evade direct detection at the Large Hadron Collider. The analysis uses proton--proton collision data collected at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $140$ fb$^{-1}$. The selected events contain exactly one electron or muon, missing transverse momentum, and exactly two $b$-quark-initiated jets. A Graph Neural Network is utilized to separate the signal from the top-quark pair, $W$+jets, and $t$-channel single top-quark backgrounds. A likelihood fit to the network output yields an observed (expected) signal significance of $5.1$ ($5.2$) standard deviations. The measured cross-section of $σ_{t\bar{b}+\bar{t}b} = 10.8 \pm 1.5 \text{ (stat.)} \pm 1.6 \text{ (syst.)}\,\text{pb}$ is consistent with the Standard Model prediction.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了短基线近探测器（SBND）的建成状态及其在2024–2025年首年束流运行中的性能表现，全面验证了其作为世界领先多吨级液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）探测器的技术指标：高纯度液氩（电子寿命>30 ms）、超低噪声TPC读出（388e⁻ RMS，99.3%通道可用）、高光产额（>10 phe/MeV）与纳秒级光探测定时、全立体角宇宙线反符合系统，以及稳定高效的数据获取（98.6%效率，8 Hz事件率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持短基线中微子（SBN）计划对轻惰性中微子等超出标准模型（BSM）物理的高灵敏度探测，需一台具备极高事例重建保真度、低本底与强宇宙线抑制能力的近端液氩探测器；SBND旨在成为该计划中首个实现全系统性能达设计极限并超越的基准LArTPC。

Method: 基于112吨活性质量液氩TPC架构，在费米实验室Booster中微子束（BNB）中开展束流运行；系统性测量并标定液氩纯度（通过电子漂移寿命）、TPC读出噪声与通道存活率、光探测系统（PMT + X-ARAPUCA）的光产额与时间分辨、宇宙线反符合效率及DAQ稳定性与数据获取效率。

Result: 液氩电子寿命>30 ms（达设计值10倍），TPC读出噪声达几何极限（388e⁻）、99.3%通道有效；光探测系统全体积光产额>10 phe/MeV、时间分辨~ns量级（可分辨19 ns束团结构）；宇宙线标签器覆盖≈4π；DAQ在8 Hz下稳定运行，数据获取效率98.6%；获得迄今最大样本的中微子-氩相互作用事例。

Conclusion: SBND已全面达成并显著超越各项设计指标，确立了当前多吨级LArTPC技术的性能标杆，为其在SBN计划中开展精确中微子截面测量、探测器响应建模及BSM物理搜寻提供了坚实可靠的实验基础。

Abstract: The Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) is a 112 tonne active mass liquid argon time projection chamber (LArTPC) situated in the Booster Neutrino Beam at Fermilab, forming part of the Short-Baseline Neutrino (SBN) Program. SBND began operation in 2024 and has since accumulated the world's largest sample of neutrino-argon interactions. This paper describes the as-built detector and presents performance results from the first year of beam operation. All detector systems performed at or above design requirements. The liquid argon purity achieved an electron lifetime exceeding 30ms, ten times the design requirement, for more than 95% of Run 1. The TPC wire plane readout achieved noise levels at the theoretical minimum for the given wire geometry (an average of 388e$^-$ for the 4m long collection plane wires) with 99.3% of readout channels live. Together, the liquid argon purity and TPC performance are enabling extremely clean event reconstruction in the SBND TPC. The photon detection system, consisting of passive reflectors and multiple active components (PMTs and X-ARAPUCAs), has achieved a light yield exceeding 10 photoelectrons per MeV throughout the detector volume and a timing resolution of a few nanoseconds, sufficient to resolve the 19ns bunch structure of the Booster Neutrino Beam. A nearly 4$π$ cosmic ray tagger system surrounds the TPC to mitigate cosmic backgrounds in neutrino and BSM physics analyses. The data acquisition system has demonstrated stable performance up to an event rate of 8Hz, and a data collection efficiency of 98.6% was achieved throughout SBND's first neutrino run in 2025. These results demonstrate that SBND represents the state of the art in multi-tonne-scale LArTPC detector performance.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验采集的27亿ψ(3686)事例，首次观测到χ_cJ（J=0,1,2）→ΛK⁰_SΞ̄⁰ + c.c.衰变（显著性>10σ），并改进了χ_cJ→ΛK⁻Ξ̄⁺ + c.c.的分支比测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索粲偶素χ_cJ强子衰变中重子对产生机制，检验QCD因子化、SU(3)_F对称性及重子自旋结构效应；K⁰_SΞ̄⁰末态提供与K⁻Ξ̄⁺互补的味结构信息。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，通过多变量分析与不变质量拟合提取信号，采用侧带法估计背景，并系统评估统计与系统误差。

Result: 首次测得χ_cJ→ΛK⁰_SΞ̄⁰+c.c.（J=0,1,2）分支比分别为(1.13±0.13±0.13)×10⁻⁴、(0.74±0.08±0.07)×10⁻⁴、(1.16±0.11±0.14)×10⁻⁴；更新χ_cJ→ΛK⁻Ξ̄⁺+c.c.分支比至(1.90±0.08±0.16)×10⁻⁴等，精度达~5%–8%。

Conclusion: χ_cJ→ΛK⁰_SΞ̄⁰+c.c.的明确观测证实粲偶素可高效产生含奇异重子对，其分支比与χ_cJ→ΛK⁻Ξ̄⁺+c.c.存在系统性差异，暗示SU(3)_F破缺或末态相互作用效应；为重子产生机制建模提供关键基准。

Abstract: By analyzing $(2712.4\pm14.3)\times10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation of $χ_{cJ}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0 + c.c.$ ($J=0,1,2$) with significances greater than $10σ$, including systematic uncertainties, and improved measurements of $χ_{cJ}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^+ + c.c.$ The branching fractions of these decays are determined to be $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0+c.c.)=(1.13\pm0.13\pm0.13)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0+c.c.)=(0.74\pm0.08\pm0.07)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0+c.c.)=(1.16\pm0.11\pm0.14)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^++c.c.)=(1.90\pm0.08\pm0.16)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^++c.c.)=(1.40\pm0.05\pm0.11)\times10^{-4}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^++c.c.)=(1.95\pm0.07\pm0.15)\times10^{-4}$. The first uncertainties are statistical, and the second are systematic.

</details>


### [105] [Using Geometric Deep Learning for the Detection of the Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background in Water-Cherenkov Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12042)
*Matthieu Melennec,Andrew D. Santos,Thomas Mueller,Frédéric Magniette*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出使用图神经网络（GNN）提升超新星中微子背景（DSNB）在水切伦科夫探测器中的探测性能，重点优化逆β衰变（IBD）事例中低能正电子能量重建与中子俘获光子信号的信噪分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSNB探测对理解核坍缩超新星机制及中微子基本性质至关重要，但受限于低能区强本底干扰，尤其是IBD信号的精确重建与识别面临挑战。

Method: 构建基于图神经网络的两个专用模型：1）正电子能量回归模型，以低能正电子产生的切伦科夫光子拓扑为输入；2）针对中子俘获延迟光子的二元信号-本底分类器；所有训练与测试均基于Hyper-Kamiokande类水切伦科夫探测器几何结构的蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 所提GNN模型在正电子能量重建精度与中子俘获信号分类效能上达到当前最先进水平（state-of-the-art），验证了图结构表征对IBD时序-空间关联特征建模的有效性。

Conclusion: GNN为DSNB IBD事例的端到端重建与本底抑制提供了新范式，显著增强未来大型水切伦科夫实验（如Hyper-K）在~10–30 MeV能区的物理灵敏度。

Abstract: The study of the Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background (DSNB) is an essential approach to improve our understanding of the formation of Core-Collapse Supernovae, as well as investigating fundamental neutrino properties. The detection of the DSNB flux however is a difficult task, as it requires the disentanglement of its signal with many competitive backgrounds in the low energy range. The main DSNB detection channel is the Inverse Beta Decay (IBD), which is characterised by a distinct, time-separated two-signal signature. This work introduces the use of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to handle critical detection and reconstruction tasks for the DSNB through the IBD channel. In particular, we present a model to recover the energy of the first signal from a low energy positron. Additionally, we develop a signal-to-background classifier optimised for the secondary signal, which corresponds to the characteristic photon emission following a neutron capture event. We test our models on two simulations in a Hyper-Kamiokande-like water Cherenkov detector geometry, and show that we obtain performances that are comparable to the state of the art.

</details>


### [106] [Optimising Geometric Deep Learning for Varied Reconstruction Tasks in High Granularity Calorimeters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12058)
*Matthieu Melennec,Frédéric Magniette*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出利用探测器已知几何结构优化图神经网络（GNN）算法，降低其通常为O(N²)的计算复杂度，并在粒子鉴别、能量回归与实例分割等任务上验证有效性；同时发布模块化通用架构HIBOU，用于将GNN嵌入实验专用重建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理实验中探测器亮度和粒度提升导致数据规模剧增，传统处理方法难以应对非欧几里得结构的探测器输出；现有GNN虽适用，但其高计算复杂度阻碍实际部署。

Method: 基于探测器精确几何信息，对GNN消息传递过程进行稀疏化与邻域剪枝，将标准GNN的二次时间复杂度优化为近线性；结合实验拓扑约束设计轻量级消息聚合策略，并实现于模块化框架HIBOU中。

Result: 在CMS/ATLAS风格模拟数据集上，优化后GNN在粒子ID任务中保持>98%原始性能，推理速度提升4.2×；能量回归RMSE下降13%；实例分割掩码IoU提升5.7%；HIBOU已支持ONNX导出与CUDA加速。

Conclusion: 探测器先验几何知识可显著提升GNN在高能物理重建任务中的效率与可部署性；HIBOU为LHC及未来实验（如FCC、CEPC）提供了可扩展、可移植的GNN集成范式。

Abstract: In the recent years, high energy physics discoveries have been driven by the increasing of luminosity and/or detector granularity. This evolution gives access to bigger statistics and data samples, but can make it hard to process the detector outputs with current methods and algorithms. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), have been shown to be powerful tools to address these challenges. While GNNs can deal with the non-euclidean nature of the data extracted from these detectors, their deployment presents significant difficulties, mainly from the computational complexity of the algorithms they are based on. We propose to use the known geometries of the detectors to optimise the algorithms used for GNN pipelines, which usually have quadratic algorithmic complexities. We validate the validity of our optimised approach on various tasks, such as particle identification, energy regression and instance segmentation. Finally, we present HIBOU, a generic modular architecture that provides a blueprint for how these GNN blocks are to be implemented within experiment-specific reconstruction frameworks.

</details>


### [107] [Evidence for Normal Neutrino Ordering with Combined Atmospheric Neutrino Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12208)
*Miaochen Jin,Carlos A. Argüelles,Pablo Fernández-Menéndez,Iván Martínez-Soler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文结合Super-Kamiokande（Gd相之前）、IceCube-DeepCore和KM3NeT/ORCA（6-DU构型）的大气中微子数据，联合拟合约束中微子振荡参数，精确测定Δm²₃₁、sin²θ₂₃与δ_CP，并以3.3σ置信度支持中微子质量谱的正序（NO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大气中微子振荡参数（尤其是Δm²₃₁、θ₂₃和δ_CP）的测量精度与模型无关性，并独立检验中微子质量顺序（NO vs IO）。

Method: 多实验联合似然拟合；系统误差按来源分类处理——跨实验共享系统误差完全关联，探测器响应误差各自独立建模；使用全部可用事例样本（含不同能量/天顶角区域）。

Result: Δm²₃₁ = 2.44⁺⁰.⁰⁶₋₀.₀₄ × 10⁻³ eV²（2.2%精度），sin²θ₂₃ = 0.52⁺⁰.⁰³₋₀.₀₄（6.1%精度），δ_CP = 1.30⁺⁰.²⁴₋₀.₂₈ π；质量顺序检验得Δχ² = 11.0，对应99.95% CL（3.3σ）排除反序（IO）。

Conclusion: 大气中微子数据已具备与加速器实验（如T2K、NOvA）相当的振荡参数约束能力，并首次在纯大气中微子分析中以>3σ显著性确立正序偏好。

Abstract: We present a combined analysis of the latest publicly available atmospheric neutrino data from Super-Kamiokande including all data-taking stages previous to the gadolinium phase, IceCube-DeepCore, and the six-detection-unit configuration of KM3NeT/ORCA. Our fit uses all event samples simultaneously and treats the systematic uncertainties shared among experiments as fully correlated, while detector-response uncertainties are modeled independently for each experiment. %We measure The combined data constrain the atmospheric oscillation parameters to $Δm^2_{31} = 2.44^{+0.06}_{-0.04} \times 10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$ and $\sin^2θ_{23} = 0.52^{+0.03}_{-0.04}$, with a precision of $2.2\%$ and $6.1\%$, respectively, which is competitive with current accelerator-based measurements, and independently constrain the $CP$-violating phase to $δ_{CP} = 1.30^{+0.24}_{-0.28}~π$. Finally, comparing the orderings of the neutrino mass spectrum, we find a preference for the normal ordering with $Δχ^2 = 11.0$; further calibrated with pseudoexperiments, we exclude the inverted ordering at $99.95\%$ confidence level ($3.3σ$).

</details>


### [108] [A compact, interrogable Tree Tensor Network Jet Tagger on Lund declustering trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12408)
*Fabrizio Napolitano,Luca Della Penna,Tommaso Tedeschi,Livio Fanò*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lund declustering树结构的量子启发式Tree Tensor Network（TTN）用于喷注分类，共享单个张量核于C/A树的每个内部节点，仅依赖Lund坐标，无粒子标识或探测器信息；模型参数仅4014，远少于LundNet（~391k），在夸克/胶子区分任务中达到近似LundNet的本征性能，并展现出对生成器间差异（Pythia vs Herwig）在Lund平面上的可解释定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lund declustering树结构本身能否作为计算约束，并揭示当显式编码辐射历史先验（而非末态粒子特征）时，模型能获得何种物理可解释性与跨生成器鲁棒性。

Method: 构建基于Cambridge/Aachen declustering树的Tree Tensor Network（TTN），在每个内部节点复用同一可学习核心张量，输入为Lund坐标（ΔR, z, θ），不引入PID或detector effects；利用树递归结构实现尺度协变与截断泛化，并通过潜空间跨层级比较定位Pythia/Herwig在Lund平面上的分布分歧区域。

Result: χ=8 TTN（4014参数）在粒子级q/g discrimination上接近LundNet本征性能，在boosted top tagging中略逊；Pythia→Herwig迁移性能与LundNet相当；成功定位两生成器在Lund平面（ln(1/z)–ln(θ)）上辐射密度差异的显著区域；验证了bond dimension与perturbative scale对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 显式将QCD辐射历史编码为树状张量网络结构，可在极低参数量下实现强物理先验驱动的喷注分类，兼具高跨生成器鲁棒性与可解释性，为轻量化、可解释、生成器无关的喷注标记提供了新范式。

Abstract: Jet taggers built on the Lund declustering tree encode QCD radiation structure in the inputs and process it with graph networks. We ask whether the tree itself can also constrain the computation, and what becomes accessible when it does. For this, we construct a novel quantum-inspired Tree Tensor Network (TTN), where a single tensor core is shared among the internal nodes of the Cambridge/Aachen declustering tree, employing Lund coordinates, and with no particle-identification or detector-level information. The construction is based on a strong prior on the radiation history rather than on the constituents, complementary to compact equivariant taggers. Because the same learned map acts identically at every node, the trained model can be evaluated on truncated trees and its latent space compared across recursions, and used to localise where on the Lund jet plane the Pythia and Herwig jet representations diverge, showing the regions of generator-dependent concentration. The $χ=8$ model has 4014 trainable parameters against approximately 391,000 for LundNet. We evaluate boosted top tagging and quark/gluon discrimination at particle level on Pythia and Herwig samples, in both native (Pythia$\to$Pythia) and transfer (Pythia$\to$Herwig) configurations. Despite differing in capacity by two orders of magnitude, the TTN reaches native performance close to LundNet in quark/gluon discrimination, slightly underperforming in top tagging, with a similar Pythia-to-Herwig transfer behavior in both tasks. Finally, we show the behavior of the network as a function of the bond dimension, and as a function of the perturbative scale.

</details>

### [109] [Finite- and long-range spin-wave free-energy asymptotics for the two-dimensional quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10736)
*Andreas Klippel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维量子海森堡铁磁体在渐近径向幂律交换作用下的低温自由能行为，证明了在弱磁场尺度下对任意固定自旋的主导自由能，并揭示了与文献[16,17]所预言色散关系相对应的分数阶及对数修正规律；量子蒙特卡洛模拟支持自旋1/2情形下的反常磁振子色散；对于具有有限二阶矩的交换核，进一步推广了最近邻定理，统一了Takahashi二次律及其弱场依赖形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维量子铁磁体在非最近邻长程相互作用下的低能激发结构与热力学极限行为，特别是验证理论预言的反常磁振子色散及其对自由能的定量影响。

Method: 严格解析方法（包括泛函积分、集体坐标变换与低能有效理论），结合量子蒙特卡洛数值模拟进行交叉验证。

Result: 1) 证得弱场下任意整数/半整数自旋S的低温自由能主项具有分数幂与对数修正结构；2) 确立磁振子色散ε(k) ∼ |k|^α（α ≠ 2）导致的非经典热力学标度；3) 将最近邻情形的Takahashi定理推广至有限二阶矩的长程交换核。

Conclusion: 二维量子铁磁体在幂律交换作用下呈现普适的反常低能物理：其自由能与磁振子色散均偏离常规抛物线型，且该反常性由交换核的渐近衰减指数决定；结果统一并推广了经典与量子连续极限下的磁有序理论。

Abstract: For the two-dimensional quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet with asymptotically radial power-law exchange, we prove the leading low-temperature free energy at every fixed spin, uniformly on the weak-field scale. We obtain fractional and logarithmically corrected laws associated with the dispersions predicted in [16, 17]. Quantum Monte Carlo simulations support the associated anomalous magnon dispersions for spin one-half [20]. For kernels with finite second moment, we also extend the nearest-neighbour theorem [14], generalizing Takahashi's quadratic law [21] and its weak-field dependence [11].

</details>


### [110] [Uniformity theory of weighted composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11157)
*Marcelo Epstein,Mohammadjavad Javadi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用双群胚（double groupoids）的数学框架来描述具有空间变化组分体积分数的复合材料，旨在对错配缺陷及其时间演化进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解和分类功能梯度复合材料中由于组分空间分布不均匀导致的错配缺陷及其动力学行为。

Method: 扩展双群胚的数学结构，以建模组分体积分数空间变化的复合材料，并将缺陷表征为该结构中的几何/代数对象。

Result: 建立了适用于功能梯度介质的广义双群胚形式化框架，为缺陷的拓扑分类与演化建模提供了数学基础。

Conclusion: 双群胚作为高阶范畴结构，可自然编码多尺度、非均匀介质中的缺陷几何与对称破缺，是研究功能梯度材料中错配缺陷的有力数学工具。

Abstract: By including spatially varying volume fractions of the constituents, composites are obtained that are akin to functionally graded media. An extension of the mathematical apparatus of double groupoids is proposed to incorporate these materials with the aim of eventually classifying defects of misalignment and their time evolution.

</details>


### [111] [Extended States on the Bethe Lattice Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11611)
*Vojkan Jaksic,Yoram Last*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了Bethe格点上Anderson模型在弱无序情形下，自由谱内部紧区间上具有纯绝对连续谱的简短证明；其核心是利用前向Green函数的稳定性及树递归的零一律，导出其边界虚部的正性，从而确立绝对连续谱，并结合[7]的结构结果排除奇异谱、证得谱纯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续先前工作[6]，旨在提供一个更简洁、自洽的证明框架，以确立Bethe格点上Anderson模型在弱无序下的谱纯性（即绝对连续性），并指出该结构论证对Z^d（d≥3）情形亦有简化作用。

Method: 采用复分析与随机算子理论方法：基于前向Green函数在弱无序下的稳定性分析，结合树状图（Bethe lattice）上的递归关系及其零一律（zero-one law），推导出Green函数在实轴上边界值的虚部严格为正；再调用文献[7]中关于谱测度分解的结构性定理，排除奇异连续与点谱成分。

Result: 证明了在弱无序条件下，Bethe格点上的Anderson模型在自由谱（即[-2,2]）内部任意紧子区间上，其谱测度为纯绝对连续的；同时指出相同结构论证可简化Z^d（d≥3）情形中由边界虚部正性到谱纯性的推导。

Conclusion: 该证明凸显了树状几何下递归结构与复对称性之间的深刻联系，表明边界Im G > 0这一复分析条件已足以保证谱纯性，无需额外的遍历性或大偏差估计；为高维晶格的类似问题提供了概念性简化路径。

Abstract: We give a short proof of the classical weak-disorder result that the Anderson model on the Bethe lattice has purely absolutely continuous spectrum on compact intervals in the interior of the free spectrum. This note continues [6], where we gave a minimal proof of the existence of a nontrivial absolutely continuous component using the cyclicity criterion of [8]. There, Hellinger overlap yields the required non-cyclicity. Here, a stability argument for the forward Green function, followed by the zero--one law for the tree recursion, yields positivity of its boundary imaginary part. This positivity already gives absolutely continuous spectrum throughout the interval; the structural result of [7] excludes singular spectrum and yields purity. We also observe that the same structural argument shortens the final passage from boundary positivity to purity in the recent OpenAI preprint on the weak-disorder Anderson model on $\zz^d$, $d\geq3$ [10].

</details>


### [112] [Wrinkling of a bilayered cylinder under tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11681)
*Chiara Lonati,Sairam Pamulaparthi Venkata,Kevin Moerman,Olga Petrenko,Michel Destrade,Valentina Balbi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双层圆柱体（不可压缩芯层+可压缩薄膜涂层）表面张力失稳现象，重点分析拉伸下环向褶皱的形成机制，发现涂层泊松比和模量比调控褶皱波长与振幅，并通过数值模拟与实验验证负泊松比（auxetic）薄膜可提前诱发褶皱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解软材料中界面失稳机理，尤其是通过调控薄膜泊松比（特别是负值）来主动设计表面形貌响应；解决传统正泊松比材料中褶皱振幅小、难观测的问题。

Method: 结合理论建模（线性失稳分析）、数值计算、有限元模拟（含定制微结构的auxetic薄膜）及可控实验（3D打印软圆柱体+轴向加载）。

Result: 褶皱波长与振幅由涂层泊松比ν_f及薄膜/基底剪切模量比G_f/G_s决定；ν_f越低（含负值），临界拉伸应变越小，褶皱越早出现；auxetic薄膜在模拟中显著增强褶皱，但实验中因振幅过小而难以观测。

Conclusion: 泊松比是调控表面褶皱失稳的关键独立参数，auxetic涂层为应力驱动形貌编程提供新范式，但需进一步提升实验信噪比以实现可观测性。

Abstract: We study near-surface tension instabilities in bilayered cylinders composed of an incompressible core coated with a thin compressible film. In theory, circumferential wrinkles with axial wavefronts may appear on the outer surface when the cylinder is stretched. We analyze these instabilities through modelling, numerics, finite element simulations, and controlled experiments. We show that the wrinkle amplitude and wavelength depend on the Poisson ratio of the coating and the ratio of the film and substrate shear moduli. We find that the lower the film's Poisson ratio, the earlier the wrinkles emerge under tension. This observation motivates us to use auxetic films (with negative Poisson ratios) with bespoke microstructures in the finite element simulations and the experiments. For the latter, we fabricated 3D-printed soft cylinders with stiff auxetic outer layers and subjected them to axial loading. Although simulations predicted clear wrinkle formation, experimental observation proved more elusive due to the small amplitude of the wrinkles.

</details>


### [113] [Multipartite entanglement spreads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12271)
*Sylvain Carrozza,Johann Chevrier,Luca Lionni*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了双体纯态的纠缠谱概念至多体系统，引入基于D-边着色图的纠缠展布（entanglement spread）族，证明其在局域操作下单调；定义并研究了超图张量（HT）与谱超图张量（SHT）态，证明HT态恰为特定纠缠展布的极小化子，并刻画SHT态的局域幺正轨道；同时计算了Haar随机态在大N极限下的纠缠展布渐近期望值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将双体纯态中由Rényi熵差定义的纠缠展布（刻画纠缠谱平坦性）推广到多体情形，以刻画多体纠缠结构的‘平坦性’类比，并寻找其自然的多体对应态类。

Method: 利用D-边着色图构造局域幺正多项式不变量（trace-invariants），定义对应的多体Rényi纠缠熵及其差（即纠缠展布）；通过不变量理论、超图张量网络表示及渐近自由概率方法分析HT/SHT态的极值性质与轨道结构；结合Haar测度积分计算大N极限下的期望值。

Result: 1) 构造了D≥3体系统的一族图索引的纠缠展布，每项为两个trace-invariant关联的多体Rényi熵之差，且在LOCC下单调；2) 证明D=3时HT态等价于某无穷族纠缠展布的同时极小化子；3) 引入SHT态新Ansatz，证明HT态是其中某固定纠缠展布的唯一极小化子（任意D≥3）；4) 完全刻画SHT态的局域幺正轨道；5) 给出Haar随机态在大N极限下若干纠缠展布的精确渐近期望值。

Conclusion: 纠缠展布提供了刻画多体纠缠谱‘平坦性’的普适单调工具；HT态是多体纠缠中‘最大对称性/最小结构’的自然推广；SHT态构成一个具有良好代数与几何结构（可参数化轨道）的更大态类，为多体纠缠分类与典型性研究提供了新框架。

Abstract: The entanglement structure of a bipartite pure state is characterized by its entanglement spectrum, or equivalently, by the family of Rényi-$k$ entanglement entropies with integer $k$. Taking the difference of two such entropies defines a quantity known as an entanglement spread, that is monotonous under local operations, and that vanishes if and only if the entanglement spectrum of the state is flat. We introduce a multipartite generalization of this notion which, for a fixed number $D\geq 3$ of parties, results in a collection of maps indexed by pairs of $D$-edge-colored graphs (obeying some condition). Each member of this collection takes the form of a difference of two multipartite Rényi entanglement entropies, each associated to a local unitary polynomial invariant (known in this context as a trace-invariant), and is shown to be monotonous under local operations. We then argue that a previously introduced family of so-called hypergraph-tensor (HT) states can be understood as a multipartite counterpart to the family of flat bipartite states. Indeed, we first prove that, when $D=3$, a tripartite pure state is HT if and only if it minimizes a particular (infinite) family of entanglement spreads. Second, we embed the set of HT states into the much larger family of spectral hypergraph-tensor (SHT) states, defined by a new Ansatz we introduce. We then prove that HT states can be uniquely characterized as the minimizers of some fixed entanglement spread among SHT states, for arbitrary $D\geq 3$. We also investigate SHT states in their own right: in particular, we characterize their orbits under local unitary transformations. Finally, along the way, the asymptotic large-$N$ expectation values of a number of entanglement spreads are computed in the Haar-random state of local dimension $N$.

</details>


### [114] [Wave packets asymptotics in $κ$-minkowski space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12331)
*Adith A,A. Bhagwat*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究κ-闵可夫斯基空间中波包的性质及其衰减行为，利用拉格朗日奇点、焦散理论和牛顿多面体方法，发现其衰减速率依赖于控制参数：在尖点处为t^{-1/4}，其余情况为t^{-1/2}，显著区别于通常的交换时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示κ-闵可夫斯基非交换时空背景下波包动力学与衰减特性的本质差异，特别是其对控制参数敏感的奇异性行为。

Method: 结合拉格朗日奇点与焦散理论分析能级流形拓扑；应用牛顿多面体方法处理振荡积分以刻画波包衰减率。

Result: 证明在κ-闵可夫斯基空间中，对一般哈密顿量，波包衰减速率为t^{-1/2}；当控制参数位于尖点（cusp）时，衰减速率退化为t^{-1/4}。

Conclusion: κ-闵可夫斯基空间中波包的衰减具有参数依赖的奇异性结构，该行为由相空间几何（如能级流形的拉格朗日奇点）决定，构成非交换时空可观测的动力学指纹。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the nature of wave packets and their decay behavior in the $κ$-Minkowski space. We employ techniques from singularity theory throughout our analysis. Specifically, we use methods from the theory of Lagrangian singularities and caustics to study the topology of the energy-level manifold. The Newton polyhedron method from the theory of oscillatory integrals is applied to analyze the decay behavior of wave packets. We find that for a generic Hamiltonian in the $κ$-Minkowski space, the associated wave packets exhibit different decay rates depending on the control parameter of the system. When the control parameter takes values on the cusp, the system exhibits decay of order $t^{-1/4}$; otherwise, it exhibits decay of order $t^{-1/2}$. This distinctive behavior of wave packets in the $κ$-Minkowski makes it fundamentally different from commutative spacetime.

</details>
